# Log

Append-only record of every operation on the wiki, and the only record of which Sources have been ingested. Each entry header is `## YYYY-MM-DD | operation | label` (grep with `^## `). The body lists each raw path with its outcome: `ingested → pages touched`, `skipped: <reason>` or `non-conforming`.

Note: Cluster 09 (ticket 09). Created causes/air-in-fuel-system, bad-glow-plugs, bad-recoil-starter, empty-fuel-tank, fuel-valve-off, incorrect-starting-procedure, wet-electrical-components and 12 Procedures (clean-or-replace-the-air-filter, clean-the-carburetor, rebuild-the-carburetor, replace-the-carburetor, replace-the-flywheel-key, replace-the-fuel-filter, replace-the-fuel-line, replace-the-ignition-coil, start-a-mower-with-old-gas, start-a-zero-turn-mower, test-the-ignition-coil, tune-up); updated the Problem, the 12 existing Causes and procedures/replace-the-spark-plug. Dropped causes/vapor-lock: no Source notes support it. The Problem now has 120 Sources and power_sources gas, diesel (diesel-lawn-mower-wont-start); its Possible causes table was re-ranked from causes-named lines (most Sources are general "won't start" articles that also list no-crank causes). Difficulty inferred where the Sources gave none: replace-the-fuel-line, start-a-zero-turn-mower, test-the-ignition-coil. time_estimate "not stated by the Sources" on clean-the-carburetor, replace-the-fuel-line, start-a-mower-with-old-gas, test-the-ignition-coil. Conflicts flagged: starter fluid vs carburetor cleaner for the intake test (Problem); oiling foam filters and air-filter intervals (clogged-air-filter, clean-or-replace-the-air-filter); clean vs replace a lightly dirty plug (fouled-spark-plug); fuel age, where to mix stabilizer, the "minimum 10% ethanol" typo and diesel shelf life (old-fuel); clean vs replace the gas cap (plugged-fuel-cap-vent); safety bar order (incorrect-starting-procedure); reusing carburetor gaskets (clean-the-carburetor); coil repair difficulty (replace-the-ignition-coil); cold-start throttle position (start-a-zero-turn-mower); service interval (tune-up). Plain text for later clusters on the Problem: bad-safety-switch, weak-or-dead-battery, bad-ignition-switch, loose-or-corroded-battery-cables, bad-starter-solenoid, faulty-charging-system, blown-fuse (engine-wont-crank), wrong-fuel and overfilled-oil (engine-starts-then-dies), bad-gas-cap-seal (mower-is-leaking-gas), water-in-fuel (engine-runs-rough), and the sibling Problems engine-wont-crank and engine-starts-then-dies; the owning clusters link them and add back-links. Named once and left out of the table: hydrolocked-engine (too-much-oil Sources; a no-crank cause), cracked-fuel-line, cracked-fuel-tank, bad-carburetor-gasket-or-needle-and-seat, bad-charger (battery mowers). Finalizer spot-check of old-fuel, fouled-spark-plug, plugged-fuel-cap-vent, dirty-carburetor and clogged-air-filter (25 random citations): 1 misplaced (old-fuel cited the John Deere turns-over article for rough running; removed). old-fuel's non-template Safety section folded into a Fix callout.

Note: Cluster 10 (ticket 10), written by parallel page writers plus a finalizer. Created problems/engine-wont-crank (102 Sources; mower_types push, riding, zero-turn; power_sources gas, diesel), causes/bad-ignition-switch, bad-safety-switch, bad-starter-motor, bad-starter-solenoid, bad-wiring-or-ground, blown-fuse, faulty-charging-system, hydrolocked-engine, loose-or-corroded-battery-cables, seized-engine, weak-or-dead-battery and 11 Procedures (charge-the-battery, jump-start-the-battery, read-a-wiring-schematic, replace-the-ignition-switch, replace-the-seat-switch, replace-the-starter-motor, replace-the-starter-solenoid, reset-the-ignition-code, test-and-replace-a-fuse, test-the-battery, test-the-starter-solenoid). No page dropped. Updated problems/engine-cranks-but-wont-start: its seven plain-text rows for this cluster's Causes are now links with sourced difficulty (counts and order unchanged), and the Related problems and Possible causes intro link engine-wont-crank; those seven Causes list that Problem with the Sources that name it there (new Sources added after their existing S-numbers). Updated the earlier-cluster Causes the engine-wont-crank notes name: incorrect-starting-procedure (13 Sources), bad-recoil-starter (3), bad-ignition-coil (2), wet-electrical-components (2). The notes name no other cluster-09 Cause (bad-fuel-pump, clogged-air-filter, clogged-fuel-filter, clogged-fuel-line, dirty-carburetor, empty-fuel-tank, fouled-spark-plug, low-compression, old-fuel, plugged-fuel-cap-vent, sheared-flywheel-key) or wrong-choke-setting for this Problem, so they are not linked. hydrolocked-engine belongs to engine-wont-crank (too-much-oil Sources: the engine won't turn with the rope or starter); counted from the three Sources whose notes name hydrolock for this Problem (Bad Boy, Kubota, Toro). "bad-battery" in the notes is counted as weak-or-dead-battery. Values inferred with no Source: difficulty on charge-the-battery (easy), read-a-wiring-schematic (moderate), reset-the-ignition-code (easy), test-and-replace-a-fuse (easy), test-the-battery (easy), test-the-starter-solenoid (moderate); time_estimate is "not stated by the Sources" on jump-start-the-battery, read-a-wiring-schematic, reset-the-ignition-code, test-and-replace-a-fuse, test-the-battery, test-the-starter-solenoid. Conflicts flagged: replace vs have the starter confirmed or rebuilt first (bad-starter-motor); baking-soda cleaning mix (loose-or-corroded-battery-cables); full-charge voltage 12.7 V vs over 12 V vs 12.6 V (weak-or-dead-battery, test-the-battery); disconnecting cables before charging and maximum charge rate (charge-the-battery); car key on or off while jumping (jump-start-the-battery). Plain text for later clusters: on engine-wont-crank, Bad PTO switch (blades-wont-engage) and Overfilled oil (engine-starts-then-dies) in the table, and the Battery keeps dying and Battery mower won't start Problems; on the new Causes, the sibling Problems they mention without pages (engine-starts-then-dies, blades-wont-engage, battery-keeps-dying, battery-mower-wont-start and others). causes/bad-wiring-or-ground carries a corded-mower tip (power cord damage, multimeter check): corded mowers have no power_sources value and need a vocabulary decision. Finalizer spot-check of 20 random citations (5 each on weak-or-dead-battery, bad-safety-switch, bad-starter-solenoid, and 5 across replace-the-starter-motor, test-the-battery, replace-the-ignition-switch, test-and-replace-a-fuse, jump-start-the-battery): 0 errors; one (replace-the-ignition-switch S4, shorted switch blows the fuse) is not in that page's notes block but is in the raw Source and was kept.

Note: Cluster 11 (ticket 11), four parallel page writers plus a finalizer. Created problems/engine-starts-then-dies (82 Sources with notes; mower_types push, self-propelled, riding, zero-turn; power_sources gas, battery), causes/dirty-cooling-fins, dirty-fuel-tank, overfilled-oil, wrong-fuel, wrong-oil-or-low-oil, procedures/change-the-engine-oil, clean-the-cooling-fins, clean-the-gas-tank, drain-excess-oil; updated causes/wrong-choke-setting (75 Sources). No page dropped. dirty-fuel-tank has one Source, which names it only for engine-cranks-but-wont-start, so it is linked there (1 of 121; that Problem now counts 121 Sources) and not on engine-starts-then-dies. Ranking counts parenthetical overheating sub-causes in causes-named lines (low oil, wrong oil, clogged deck, plugged air filter) as named; bare "clogged deck" is counted as clogged-deck-chute, which overlaps debris-on-deck-or-pulleys: the owning clusters should decide whether to merge those rows. Engine overheats is a Problem, not a Cause, so it has no table row. Carry-overs: Overfilled oil and Wrong fuel rows on engine-cranks-but-wont-start and Overfilled oil on engine-wont-crank are now links (difficulty easy, counts and order unchanged), with back-links (overfilled-oil lists engine-wont-crank from the Kubota too-much-oil Source). The existing Causes that named this Problem as plain text (dirty-carburetor, old-fuel, clogged-fuel-line, clogged-air-filter, plugged-fuel-cap-vent, clogged-fuel-filter, bad-ignition-coil, fouled-spark-plug, bad-fuel-pump, empty-fuel-tank, faulty-charging-system, bad-safety-switch, incorrect-starting-procedure) now link it, and those plus weak-or-dead-battery, bad-wiring-or-ground and fuel-valve-off list it with a "Sources for Engine starts then dies" line taken from this Problem's notes. incorrect-starting-procedure is linked from Diagnostic questions only (no causes-named line names it here). Plain text for later clusters: debris-on-deck-or-pulleys, clogged-deck-chute, dull-or-bent-blade, mowing-too-fast, wrong-engine-speed, bad-gas-cap-seal, bad-cooling-shroud, water-in-fuel, overheated-battery, battery-not-seated, bad-motor, cutting-wet-or-tall-grass; Problems engine-dies-when-hot, engine-overheats, engine-loses-power, engine-only-runs-with-choke-on, mower-is-smoking. Values inferred with no Source: difficulty easy on change-the-engine-oil, clean-the-cooling-fins, clean-the-gas-tank, drain-excess-oil; time_estimate is "not stated by the Sources" on all four. Conflicts flagged: drain to the full mark vs below max fill (overfilled-oil); topping up low oil may help vs rarely helps once shut down (wrong-oil-or-low-oil); oil change interval 25 h/yearly vs 50 h vs 100 h and break-in change 5 h vs 5-10 h vs 2-10 h (change-the-engine-oil); compressed air on fins (clean-the-cooling-fins); water as a tank-cleaning solvent (clean-the-gas-tank). Sears oil-change video shop-link IDs are only in change-the-engine-oil Brand notes, marked lower confidence. Finalizer spot-check of 15 random citations (5 each on engine-starts-then-dies, overfilled-oil, wrong-oil-or-low-oil): 0 errors; wrong-oil-or-low-oil S99 (Husqvarna won't-stay-running) comes from the Problem notes' overheating sub-causes and is supported by the raw Source.

Note: Cluster 12 (ticket 12), four parallel page writers plus a finalizer. Created problems/uneven-cut (78 Sources with notes; mower_types push, self-propelled, riding, zero-turn; power_sources gas, battery); causes/bad-blade-adapter-or-loose-blade, bad-tire (title "Bad tire or wheel"), bent-deck-or-deck-shell, blade-installed-upside-down, debris-on-deck-or-pulleys, dull-or-bent-blade, incorrect-path-overlap, mowing-too-fast, unlevel-deck, wrong-cutting-height, wrong-engine-speed, wrong-tire-pressure; procedures/level-the-deck, measure-a-mower-blade, repair-a-flat-tire, replace-a-tire, sharpen-and-replace-the-blades. Updated problems/engine-starts-then-dies: Debris on deck or pulleys, Dull or bent blade, Mowing too fast and Wrong engine speed rows and diagnostic question linked (counts unchanged, order unchanged). No pages dropped. Wrong-size and aftermarket blade content (14 vibration Sources plus the Ferris unmapped note) is folded into bad-blade-adapter-or-loose-blade. Uneven-lawn, scalping-from-terrain and lawn-rolling content skipped as lawn care. Inferred values: difficulty of measure-a-mower-blade (easy), level-the-deck (moderate) and repair-a-flat-tire (easy) is not rated by any Source; sharpen-and-replace-the-blades moderate and replace-a-tire moderate map the Sears numeric ratings (3 and 4); time_estimate "not stated by the Sources" on measure-a-mower-blade, level-the-deck and repair-a-flat-tire; Fix difficulty on the uneven-cut table and the "pro" rating of bent-deck-or-deck-shell are finalizer/writer judgement. Conflicts flagged: blade replacement interval (yearly vs every 1-3 seasons), file angle (blade slope vs 25-30 vs 45 degrees), sharpen vs replace a dull blade (dull-or-bent-blade), front-to-rear deck rake (1/8-1/2 vs 1/4-3/8 vs 1/16-1/4 inch) and deck position while measuring (level-the-deck). The "reverse threaded, also known as right-hand" blade-bolt claim (three Sources) is recorded as a Source contradiction; no thread direction is stated. Overlap: the Sources' "clogged/plugged deck" was split by extraction between debris-on-deck-or-pulleys and clogged-deck-chute; both rows stay on uneven-cut and engine-starts-then-dies, and the clogged-deck-chute owner should decide a merge. Plain text left for later clusters on uneven-cut: Bent spindle or bad spindle bearing, Worn or stretched belt, Worn pulley or idler bearing, Bent crankshaft (mower-is-vibrating cluster), Clogged deck chute, Worn blade sail; Problems Mower is vibrating, Belt keeps coming off, Mower pulls to one side. bent-deck-or-deck-shell has no Safety content in its notes. MO-DECK spray product ID (Sears shop link) and the Sears blade removal tool number are in Brand notes, marked per Source, verify against catalog. Finalizer spot-check of 15 random citations (5 each on debris-on-deck-or-pulleys, dull-or-bent-blade, uneven-cut): 0 errors.
